# FOPX PM Master UAT Checklist

This is the central register for consolidated end-to-end UAT scenarios. Feature teams add permission-positive, permission-negative, and cross-scope cases here before marking a feature implementation-complete. Runtime permission testing is performed during consolidated final UAT.


## Users–Resources–Teams–Project Team alignment — 2026-10-31

No new permission key or existing role grant is introduced. Configure test identities by effective permissions and authenticated tenant/project scope, not by role name. Project Team changes use the existing `Resource.Assign` permission (the API policy also accepts existing `Resource.Allocate` or `Resource.Manage` claims); Team-target changes also require `Team.View` or `Team.Manage`; reads use `Project.View`; stakeholder relationships and invitations use `Stakeholders.Manage`. The stakeholder Resource picker also requires a Resource-view-capable grant. Annual Budget candidate eligibility uses active same-Tenant current Project Team membership or a current Sponsor/Manager assignment, an assigned `System` or `Management` role category, and effective `ProjectAnnualBudget.Approve`; Sponsor/Manager assignment eligibility does not require a Resource profile. An approval action additionally requires an explicit pending assignment. The submitter uses `ProjectAnnualBudget.SubmitForReview`, while persistent Project defaults have their separate settings permissions. Team membership and candidate visibility alone never grant privileged permission. Host-connected recheck on 2026-10-31 verified Shady/NexusPmo (database ID 9, online), six applied migrations, and one pending alignment migration. UPT runtime tally remains: 17 in-scope scenarios, 0 executed, 0 passed, 0 failed, 17 blocked, 0 not tested. The required schema is not deployed and no separate migration authorization is present; source checks/builds and read-only database audits are not UPT runtime passes.

## Role Administration and Seeded Admin Permissions

Configure test identities by effective permissions and authenticated Tenant scope, never by role name. The 63-key Protected Core is immutable for the protected tenant Admin. New Admin provisioning receives all active, assignable, non-reserved grants except the explicit `Program.CreateAcrossPortfolios` opt-in; existing Admin roles keep optional grants as-is, and reseeding may restore missing Core only.

| ID | Scenario | Preconditions | UAT steps | Expected result | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RAS-01 | Fresh seeded Admin role | Isolated Development database has no seeded Admin role or Admin user. | Run the identity seed; inspect its full permission set, Tenant and user assignment, and EN/AR labels in Roles & Permissions. | The protected system `tenant.admin` role receives all active, assignable, non-reserved permissions in the new-Tenant initial grant set except `Program.CreateAcrossPortfolios`, which remains explicit opt-in. Its 63-key Protected Core is mandatory. Platform, CrossTenant, and SystemOwner scopes are excluded; active catalog additions follow the same policy. | Not run; owner verification pending | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| RAS-02 | Existing Admin role and optional grants across reseed | Existing protected Admin has a recorded set containing Core and optional grants, including a deliberately removed optional Project Settings grant; the user has direct Allow/Deny overrides. | Run the ordinary seed/reseed and compare role grants, overrides, membership, and audit rows before and after. | Existing grants and membership are preserved; missing Protected Core grants are restored and Core Deny overrides are reconciled according to the protected policy; removed optional grants are not restored and no optional grant is newly added. | Not run; owner verification pending | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| RAS-03 | Protected Core reconciliation and precondition failures | Verified Development target with exactly one trusted protected Admin role and its expected membership; include one missing Core key, one missing optional key, and a Core Deny. | Run the documented canonical permission reconciliation twice and compare grants, direct overrides, memberships, and audit history. Repeat against a non-Development environment, ambiguous/multiple protected roles or members, changed role identity, and a missing/retired required Core permission. | Only missing Core grants are restored; the optional permission is not restored; only Core Deny overrides are reconciled and audited; repeat is idempotent. Failed preconditions make no partial changes. | Not run; owner verification pending | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |

Grant an unheld ordinary permission to an unassigned custom role, attempt the unheld Administration key, attempt self-escalation in the actor-assigned role, and try foreign identifiers. | View-only access cannot mutate grants. Role details/counts disclose only same-Tenant permissions. `Roles.ConfigurePermissions` permits ordinary active, assignable, scoped non-Administration grants without actor possession to an unassigned custom role; Administration remains actor-authorized only. Missing actor authorization for Administration permissions denies the grant; foreign-Tenant identifiers disclose or change no data. Ordinary non-Administration custom-role grants are not capped by the effective permissions of the actor. Platform settings/provisioning policies remain unavailable to tenant Admin. | Not run; owner verification pending | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| RAS-06 | Direct permission override Tenant isolation | Tenant A administrator has the required override-management permission; Tenant B has a user with the same permission key and an existing Allow/Deny record. | Create, update, and delete an override for Tenant A; then substitute Tenant B user/override IDs in route and body, including a stale or malformed target. | Only the authenticated Tenant's target user and override can change; foreign identifiers disclose no record and make no mutation or misleading success audit. ## Development Admin bootstrap and first-login recovery

The recovery command is a local Development operator action protected by the Development environment, verified SQL target, and SQL Server write access. Backups are optional for supported Development/UAT operations and are not an authorization or execution prerequisite. It is not a tenant API action, so no application permission key or role grant is added.

| ID | Scenario | Preconditions | UAT steps | Expected result | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ADM-BOOT-01 | Bootstrap expiry and seed idempotency | Fresh isolated Development seed uses a secure bootstrap password; separately prepare an established Admin hash and role/grant snapshot. | Inspect temporary expiry and `MustChangePassword`; sign in with expired and missing-expiry bootstrap state; rerun ordinary data seed with a different bootstrap secret; compare credential and role/grant state. | New bootstrap expiry follows `Security:LocalPasswords:TemporaryPasswordExpiryHours`; missing or elapsed expiry is rejected without issuing a session; the Admin must change a valid temporary password; reseed does not rotate an established hash or reset its first-login state/expiry or role grants. | Automated lifecycle and seed regressions passed; live UAT deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| ADM-BOOT-02 | Local Development recovery and complete login flow | Explicit Development environment, verified `Shady/NexusPmo` identity, no pending migrations, and SQL Server write access. Backup is optional. | Run `--recover-development-admin-password`; enter both passwords at the hidden console prompts; inspect the credential-free audit event; verify temporary login, forced password change, and permanent login. Repeat with Production, wrong target, expired/missing expiry, and mismatched password confirmations. | Environment, target, migration, and credential preconditions fail closed. Recovery preserves roles/grants, sets `MustChangePassword=true` and the configured expiry before first login, rejects normal API use until change, revokes old sessions, and succeeds with the new permanent password after the initial change. No secret is echoed or recorded. | Live recovery not run in that execution; a backup path was unavailable then. Backup is not a current blocker; live UAT remains deferred. |
| ADM-BOOT-03 | Development Admin temporary-password bypass | Seeded Admin has a valid local password hash, `MustChangePassword=true`, and an expired or missing temporary expiry. | Enable `Security:LocalPasswords:DevelopmentAdminTemporaryPasswordBypassEnabled`; verify login, `/api/auth/me`, refresh, and ordinary API access; verify a non-Admin account and Production are unaffected; disable the setting without changing database state and repeat the access checks. | Existing password verification remains required. Only the seeded Development Admin is exempt while enabled; returned state permits normal UI access; database credential fields and grants remain unchanged; disabling the setting restores expiry/forced-change enforcement on subsequent requests. | Automated bypass, scope, and Production regressions passed; live Development UAT remains |

### Reference Data Administration permissions — source implementation; runtime UAT pending

| ID | Scenario | Preconditions | UAT steps | Expected result | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RAS-RD-01 | Granular Reference Data grants | Ordinary roles separately assigned ReferenceData.View/Create/Edit/Delete; protected tenant Admin initialized from the current active Administration catalog. | Open roles editor; save each ordinary-role grant separately; inspect Admin lock state; attempt Admin remove/Deny and bulk replacement. | Ordinary roles save individual grants. Admin retains all eligible tenant Administration grants; locked grants cannot be removed, denied, or dropped in bulk. Platform-only capabilities are excluded. | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| RAS-RD-02 | Tenant, Platform, and integrity protection | Two tenants, Platform-managed/system values, referenced and unused tenant reference rows. | Try authorized and unauthorized CRUD/deactivation via UI and direct API, including foreign-tenant IDs, immutable values, and referenced records. | Exact permission and tenant checks apply. Platform/foreign records stay protected; deletion follows existing soft-delete/deactivation rules; referenced/immutable values remain intact and audited. | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |

## Tenant Fiscal Year Configuration Security

The feature uses `settings.read` for reads and `Settings.ManageTenantConfiguration` for tenant writes. Legacy `settings.write` is inactive and is not a write grant. No permission key or role grant is added by the fiscal-year feature. Configure test users by effective permissions and Tenant membership; do not use a role name as authorization evidence.

| ID | Scenario | Preconditions | UAT steps | Expected result | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FY-SEC-01 | Authorized read | Tenant A user has `settings.read`. | Open Tenant Settings and request the fiscal-year preview. | Only Tenant A's configured month and calculated period are displayed. | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| FY-SEC-02 | Read without write | Tenant A user has `settings.read`, not `Settings.ManageTenantConfiguration`. | View the page, then attempt to save a changed month through the UI and direct API. | Preview is readable; controls are read-only; direct write is forbidden; no setting or audit record changes. | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| FY-SEC-03 | Authorized update | Tenant A user has `Settings.ManageTenantConfiguration` and required read access; no fiscal-dependent history exists. | Change and save the start month. | Only Tenant A's Tenant-scoped value changes; audit contains actor, UTC time, Tenant, and previous/new month. | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| FY-SEC-04 | Read denial | Authenticated Tenant A user lacks `settings.read`. | Call `GET /api/settings/tenant` and `GET /api/settings/tenant/fiscal-year`; open the settings workspace. | API reads are forbidden; the workspace discloses no fiscal-year setting or preview. | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| FY-SEC-05 | Write and reset denial | Authenticated Tenant A user lacks `Settings.ManageTenantConfiguration`. | Call Tenant update and reset endpoints for `financial.fiscalYearStartMonth`. | Both operations are forbidden; configuration and audit history remain unchanged. | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| FY-SEC-06 | Organization administrator boundary | User has Organization settings access but lacks Tenant `Settings.ManageTenantConfiguration`. | Attempt to update the Tenant setting and attempt an Organization-scoped override. | Tenant write is forbidden; Organization override is rejected because this setting is Tenant-only. | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| FY-SEC-07 | Cross-Tenant isolation | Tenants A and B have different months; each test user is authorized only in their Tenant. | Authenticate as Tenant A and attempt to read/update Tenant B by supplying its ID in query, path, or body; repeat for Tenant B. | Tenant identity comes from the authenticated claim; no cross-Tenant read or write succeeds. | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| FY-SEC-08 | Unsupported scope attempts | Tenant user has `Settings.ManageTenantConfiguration`. | Attempt writes/resets through Platform, Organization, Project, and User scopes. | All unsupported scopes are rejected; no override is persisted. | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| FY-SEC-09 | Roles & Permissions catalog | Role administrator is authorized and holds the permissions being delegated. | View Settings permissions in English and Arabic and inspect assignable entries. | `settings.read` and `Settings.ManageTenantConfiguration` appear under Administration with accurate bilingual labels; inactive `settings.write` is not assignable; only delegable permissions are offered; deployment adds no grants automatically. | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| FY-SEC-10 | Role delegation boundary | Actor can manage roles but does not hold a settings permission. | Attempt to include that permission in a role update. | Permission is not offered and the API rejects an out-of-authority grant without modifying role assignments. | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| FY-SEC-11 | Historical data protection | Tenant has a persisted fiscal-year budget record, including soft-deleted history. | Attempt an authorized fiscal-year month change. | Change is blocked with an actionable message; no historical budgets, closed periods, transactions, Project budgets, or Payment Request allocations are rewritten. | Deferred to consolidated final UAT |

## Project Lifetime Ceiling and Annual Budget Lifecycle

Use effective permissions and authenticated Tenant/Project scopes for every case. Do not use role names as authorization evidence. All cases are deferred to Owner runtime UAT; source implementation did not execute runtime workflows.

|---|---|---|---|---|

| LFC-02 | Optional initial Draft | With `Project.Create` and `ProjectAnnualBudget.Create`, create a Project with an independent ceiling and current-FY Initial Annual Budget amount below the ceiling. | Project and one current-FY Annual Budget Draft persist atomically; no submission, approval, or spend authorization occurs; audit records both facts. | Owner UAT pending |
| LFC-03 | Initial Draft validation and permission denial | Try amount above ceiling, negative/over-scale amount, missing ceiling, and a caller without `ProjectAnnualBudget.Create`; inspect Project and Annual Budget records. | Each invalid request is rejected without partial Project or budget persistence; ordinary Project creation without this optional field remains available under `Project.Create`. | Owner UAT pending |
| LFC-04 | Missing budget versus Approved zero | Compare no Annual Budget, Draft amount zero, and Approved amount zero in Overview and Annual Budgets. | Three states remain distinguishable; no ceiling is displayed as annual authorization; available spend is 0 while approval status is clearly shown. | Owner UAT pending |

## Annual Budget Project approval defaults and overrides

These cases verify that Project Settings own defaults and each submission owns only its effective snapshot. On 2026-11-04, the focused API class passed 16/16, including an EF InMemory save/reload/submission snapshot regression. This does not prove SQL Server transactions or current Development persistence. Authenticated runtime UAT remains pending; current Development identity and migration history could not be read. ### Database and runtime closure recheck - 2026-11-04

The read-only EF gate verified the requested Development target as SQL Server `Shady`, database `NexusPmo`, database ID `5`. The EF model is synchronized; all 10 source migrations are recorded, including `20261101114229_AddProjectAnnualBudgetApprovalConfiguration`; there are 0 pending migrations; and the physical schema matches the current model. No migration was applied. A backup was not needed for read-only inspection, and no database write was performed.

Read-only permission counts found one active catalog row for each of `ProjectAnnualBudget.View` and `ProjectAnnualBudget.ManageApprovalConfiguration`. Manage currently has 0 role grants and 0 direct Allow/Deny overrides. View has 3 role grants, including existing Admin and Project Manager role grants; those grants were left unchanged. The protected `tenant.admin` role (1) holds `Roles.ConfigurePermissions`, `TenantAdministration.ManageScope`, and `ProjectAnnualBudget.View`; one active role member has all three and no direct Deny for them. No effective `ProjectAnnualBudget.Approve` grant was available for eligible-approver UAT.

The Development API was started temporarily in Development with startup database initialization skipped; `/health` returned `200 Healthy`, and the temporary host was stopped after the check. Playwright had no authenticated session (`about:blank`), and the health check did not exercise authenticated project APIs or SQL persistence. Consequently, no live permission grant/revoke, settings save/reload, submission inheritance, SQL Server persistence, one-time override, or cross-scope HTTP UAT was performed. The filtered P0 API classes (`ProjectSettingsAuthorizationTests`, `FinancialPermissionDelegationTests`, and `FinancialApprovalDelegationBoundaryTests`) passed 30/30 using isolated test stores. The broader filter including `PermissionCatalogArchitectureTests` ran 52 tests: 49 passed and 3 failed in that architecture class (catalog count expected 158/actual 160; `Resource.ViewCostRate` dependency assertion; Admin baseline expected 48/actual 47). Earlier test-project compile blockers were fixed in test fixtures. See [Project Settings database/runtime closure evidence](../security/permission-audit/PROJECT-SETTINGS-DATABASE-RUNTIME-CLOSURE-2026-11-04.md) for the full gate and regression record. The live scenarios below remain blocked or partial until an authorized session and eligible approver are available.

## Project Annual Budget Foundation Security

Permission checks use current effective canonical permissions plus authenticated Tenant and Project scope. Each financial workspace section has a distinct View key; each mutation, approval, settlement, reversal, closing, carry-forward, export, and persistent settings change uses its mapped operation key. `ProjectAnnualBudget.Approve` is independent from draft permissions, and `ProjectApproval.Override` is required in addition to `ProjectAnnualBudget.SubmitForReview` when changing the approval plan for one submission. The retired `finances.read` and `finances.write` keys are unsupported and do not authorize any action. No existing role is automatically granted a sensitive permission. An authorized `Roles.ConfigurePermissions` holder may grant ordinary active, assignable, scoped non-Administration permissions to a user-managed custom role without holding each key; Administration-module grants remain actor-authorized only. The target role cannot be changed to expand the effective permissions currently held by the actor.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| AB-SEC-01 | Authorized annual-budget read | User has `ProjectAnnualBudget.View` and Project A scope. | Open Project A Financial → Annual Budgets and read budget history. | Only Project A records in the authenticated Tenant are returned. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| AB-SEC-02 | Read without section permission | User lacks `ProjectAnnualBudget.View` and has no assigned-review exception. | Call the annual-budget GET endpoint directly and open the Financial tab. | API denies the list request and the UI discloses no annual-budget data. Financial Foundation read is covered by PFW-02. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| AB-SEC-03 | Draft create and edit | User has `ProjectAnnualBudget.View`, `ProjectAnnualBudget.Create`, and `ProjectAnnualBudget.EditDraft`, Project A scope, and is not in read-only View-As mode. | Create a draft, edit amounts with the current row version, then attempt an edit with a stale row version. | One record exists for Tenant/Project/FY; tenant fiscal boundaries and project currency are used; stale edits conflict; audit records commit with each successful mutation. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| AB-SEC-04 | Approval permission required | User has `ProjectAnnualBudget.View` but lacks `ProjectAnnualBudget.Approve`. | Attempt approval from the UI and directly through the API. | Approval action is unavailable in the UI and direct API returns forbidden; no status or audit change occurs. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| AB-SEC-05 | Explicit approver | User has `ProjectAnnualBudget.View` and `ProjectAnnualBudget.Approve` with Project A scope and an active assignment. | Approve a draft and retry the same request with a stale row version. | First approval succeeds once and records actor/UTC time; approved values become immutable; retry creates no duplicate event. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| AB-SEC-06 | No automatic financial approval grant | Existing roles are present before catalog seeding; an authorized role administrator has `Roles.ConfigurePermissions` and `TenantAdministration.ManageScope`. | Refresh the catalog, inspect Admin and Project Manager grants, then use Roles & Permissions to explicitly grant the scoped Annual Budget View and configuration permissions to a recipient role. The Finance workspace was consolidated so financial audit activity is inspected only from the central Audit Trail, removing redundant per-screen History UI. No financial data, audit events, approval lifecycle, calculation, permission, or navigation was changed or deleted.

Annual Budgets per fiscal year remain accessible via the selector. Approval lifecycle records remain on the budget detail. These are operational records, not redundant audit events, and are retained per §3. | Verified by source review; no code change required; browser UAT pending |

## Payment Request Annual Budget Integration Security

Payment Request reads require `ProjectPaymentRequest.View`. Draft creation, editing, submission, cancellation, and decision use their respective canonical operation keys (`ProjectPaymentRequest.Create`, `.EditDraft`, `.Submit`, `.Cancel`, and `.Approve`); each key is independently enforced with authenticated Tenant/Project scope and workflow eligibility. Self-approval is denied while no saved Payment Request exception policy exists. An approval decision also requires the current user to be the assigned active eligible Project Team approver. Reassignment requires both `ProjectPaymentRequest.Approve` and `Governance.AssignmentRecovery.Manage`, and must not approve or skip a stage. No legacy `finances.read`/`finances.write` or broad Manage fallback is valid.

| ID | Scenario | Preconditions | UAT steps | Expected result and evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PR-AB-01 | Server-derived Fiscal Year association | Tenant fiscal month is non-January; Project has current-FY Draft annual budget. | Create a Payment Request without a Tenant/FY selector; inspect its detail and audit. | API links only the current Tenant Fiscal Year budget; request stores budget ID, FY, and reference snapshot; audit records the association. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-02 | Missing annual budget | Current FY has no Project annual budget. | Attempt request creation. | Request is rejected with an actionable message; no request or audit row is created. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-03 | Draft budget submission denied | Request is linked to a Draft annual budget. | Attempt submission. | Submission is denied; it creates no approval assignment, reservation, status transition, or success audit event. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-04 | Cross-Project/Tenant reference | Two projects/tenants have annual budgets. | Tamper with route/body/detail reference to another Project or Tenant budget. | API and composite FK prevent association; no cross-scope data or audit is exposed. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-05 | Currency and fiscal-boundary validation | Project currency matches its budget; a second budget has mismatched currency or dates. | Attempt creation/submission with mismatched or prior-year budget and test the Tenant fiscal boundary. | Only a current-period matching budget can be newly submitted; currency and FY identity match Project/Tenant sources; no client-supplied Tenant/FY is trusted. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-06 | Draft/Pending/rejected reservation | Approved Annual Budget has available amount. | Create Draft, submit Pending, then reject a request; inspect availability at each state. | None of those states reserve any amount; authoritative annual balance is unchanged. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-07 | Sequential approval allocation | Sequential request has multiple approvers and enough budget. | Approve in order, attempt out-of-order action, then final approve. | Only active approver can act; rejection stops sequence; only final approval reserves once against associated FY. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-08 | Parallel Mandatory/Optional rules | Parallel request has at least one Mandatory and Optional approvers. | Approve/reject combinations, including Optional non-response and Mandatory rejection. | All Mandatory approvals required; Mandatory rejection prevents approval; Optional outcome does not block; final approval reserves once. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-09 | Amount exceeds availability | Approved annual budget has lower available balance than requested amount. | Submit and final-approve requests at and above remaining amount. | Insufficient amount is rejected from authoritative server calculation; no negative balance is produced. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-10 | Reservation idempotency | Eligible request can be final-approved. | Retry same decision/idempotency key and retry after response loss. | One final state, one allocation effect, and one business audit event exist. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-11 | Concurrent approvals | Two requests target the same Annual Budget; their total exceeds available amount. | Submit overlapping final-approval API calls against SQL Server. | At most one approval commits when combined allocations would exceed the budget; transaction and audit state are consistent. Capture SQL traces/results. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-12 | Settlement does not release allocation | Approved request has partial or full Settlement. | Record/inspect settlement and request the annual budget summary. | Original request allocation remains reserved; settlement is not deducted a second time; no automatic CostEntry is created. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-13 | Legacy approved request reconciliation | Existing Approved request has no annual-budget association. | Inspect history and attempt a new spending transition/new request that could overcommit. | Historical request/settlements/approvals remain untouched; no FY is inferred or backfilled; service blocks unsafe spending until controlled reconciliation. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-14 | Cross-year pending request | Request was submitted against FY N and remains Pending after FY boundary. | Inspect association, decisions, and attempt final approval against FY N. | Association and history remain FY N; no duplicate FY N+1 reservation or automatic transfer/cancellation occurs; only the linked Approved budget is considered. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-15 | Payment Request permission separation | User A has `ProjectPaymentRequest.View`; User B has `ProjectPaymentRequest.View` and `ProjectPaymentRequest.Create`; User C has only an unrelated or retired finance key. | Attempt reads, creates, edits, and submissions through UI and direct API. | Read requires `ProjectPaymentRequest.View`; each mutation requires its matching action key; no broad permission, role name, or legacy alias bypasses authorization. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-16 | Assigned approver permission | User has `ProjectPaymentRequest.Approve` but is unassigned, inactive, outside Project Team, or lacks an active linked resource. | Attempt approve/reject from UI and direct API. | No decision is exposed or accepted unless the actor is the assigned eligible approver; unauthorized state and audit remain unchanged. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-17 | Cross-scope access and activity | Actor is authorized only for Project A/Tenant A. | Substitute Project B/Tenant B IDs in list/detail/decision/activity routes. | No other scope's request, budget, approver, or audit activity is returned or mutated. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-18 | Shared financial audit | Perform successful create, edit, submit, assignment, decision, final approval, reservation, and pre-approval cancellation; also attempt a failing mutation. | Compare Financial Activity, `AuditEntries`, request status, and SQL transaction results. | Each successful mutation has one event with Tenant/Project, actor, UTC time, request and Annual Budget references, FY, amount/currency, and relevant before/after status/balance; failed operation has no success event; retries do not duplicate events. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-19 | Fiscal Year UI/locales | English and Arabic users, RTL and light/dark modes are available. | Inspect request list/detail, create form, annual availability and activity timeline in each mode. | Fiscal Year and budget reference are visible; amounts use Project currency; loading/error/empty states and permission action visibility are correct in EN/AR and RTL. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| PR-AB-20 | Requester cannot self-approve by default | Requester is also an eligible Project Team member and appears in a configured approver source; no saved Payment Request self-approval exception exists. | Attempt self-selection during submission, then call the submit and decision APIs directly as the requester; compare candidate lookup and `CanDecide`. | Requester is excluded from eligible approvers and cannot decide their own request through UI or API. Denial leaves state, assignments, reservation, and success-audit history unchanged; no global maker/checker restriction is inferred for other request types. | Not run; deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| PR-AB-21 | Authorized recovery for an ineligible pending approver | Pending request has exactly one undecided assignment to an approver who is now inactive or no longer eligible; replacement is active, linked to the same Project Team, and has `ProjectPaymentRequest.Approve`. Actor has both recovery permissions. | Reassign through the recovery endpoint with a current request RowVersion and reason; repeat for the current sequential stage. Inspect assignment, sequential cursor, audit, and decision behavior. | Reassignment succeeds once; replacement becomes the pending assignee/current sequential approver; the request remains Pending and no approval stage is skipped or auto-approved. Audit records assignment, old/new approvers, reason, actor, and time. | Not run; deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| PR-AB-22 | Recovery permission, eligibility, and state denial | Try with only `ProjectPaymentRequest.Approve`, only `Governance.AssignmentRecovery.Manage`, neither permission, an inactive/out-of-team/re-requester/duplicate replacement, a completed assignment, or non-Pending request. | Call the API directly for each case and inspect request, assignments, reservation, and audit. | Every invalid or unauthorized attempt is denied without partial reassignment, approval, stage skip, reservation change, or success audit; an actionable recovery reason remains available where appropriate. | Not run; deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| PR-AB-23 | Stale version, concurrent reassignment, and immutable plan | Two authorized actors attempt replacement using the same request RowVersion; original submitted plan includes ordered approvers and requirements. | Race two reassignment requests; retry with stale RowVersion; compare original submission snapshot, current assignments, sequential cursor, and audit events. | At most one reassignment commits; stale/concurrent requests conflict. The submitted approval plan snapshot remains unchanged; only a separate auditable reassignment event and current pending assignment change. | Not run; deferred to consolidated final UAT |
| PR-AB-24 | Recovery cross-Project and cross-Tenant isolation | Actor and request are scoped to Project A/Tenant A; foreign request, project, assignment, and replacement belong to Project B/Tenant B; include same-Tenant out-of-project replacement. | Substitute foreign IDs across the route and body and try a same-Tenant replacement outside the Project Team. | Foreign or out-of-project records are not disclosed or changed; replacement must be eligible within the actual Project scope. No assignment, request status, or audit mutation occurs. | Not run; deferred to consolidated final UAT |

## Actual Settlements — Financial Sprint 3

Reading settlement history requires `ProjectSettlement.View` within authenticated Tenant and Project scope. Recording a Payment Request settlement requires `PaymentSettlement.Record` and its declared `ProjectPaymentRequest.View` dependency, plus active-user and Project-scope checks. No existing role is granted the sensitive permission automatically; writes fail closed until an explicitly authorized grant exists. Supporting-document access is not added in this Sprint; correction/reversal authorization is covered in Sprint 4.

| ID | Scenario | Preconditions | UAT steps | Expected result and evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SET-01 | Record against approved request | Approved Payment Request has outstanding amount; actor has `ProjectPaymentRequest.View` and `PaymentSettlement.Record` in Project scope. | Record a positive settlement with date, unique reference, description, and idempotency key. | One PaymentSettlement with Tenant, Project, request, currency, and actor metadata is persisted; one audit event is committed in the same transaction; approval amount/history remain unchanged. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-02 | Reject non-approved requests | Requests exist in Draft, Pending, Rejected, or Cancelled state. | Attempt to record a settlement against each state through the API. | Every command is rejected; no settlement or success audit row is written. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-03 | Single full settlement | Approved amount has no earlier settlements. | Record one settlement equal to the approved amount. | Total Settled equals Approved Amount, Outstanding is zero, status is Fully Settled, and another settlement is rejected. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-04 | Multiple partial settlements | Approved request has sufficient outstanding balance. | Record multiple partial payments with distinct references and then the exact remainder. | History retains each payment; totals are accurate; status progresses Not Settled → Partially Settled → Fully Settled. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-05 | Positive amount and precision | Approved request is available. | Submit zero, negative, or more than four decimal places. | Validation rejects the command before persistence; financial values and audit history remain unchanged. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-06 | Overpayment | A request has known outstanding amount. | Submit an amount above the outstanding amount and repeat after full settlement. | Both commands conflict; total never exceeds the approved amount; no success audit event is added. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-07 | Duplicate command retry | A settlement has committed but its response was lost. | Retry the identical command with the same idempotency key, then reuse the key with a changed payload. | Exact retry returns the original settlement without another row/event; changed payload conflicts. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-08 | Duplicate settlement reference | Request already has a settlement reference. | Record another payment for that request using the same reference and another idempotency key. | Duplicate reference is rejected by application validation and the unique filtered index; no second payment is recorded. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-09 | Concurrent overpayment protection | Two commands target one Approved request; combined amounts exceed outstanding balance. | Run both commands concurrently against SQL Server. | Project-scoped serializable lock and unique/idempotency safeguards allow only a valid total; competing command conflicts; audit and settlement rows agree. Capture SQL traces and committed rows. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-10 | Currency integrity | Request currency is authoritative. | Attempt a client-supplied currency mismatch, then read settlement history. | API accepts no client currency override; stored/displayed settlement currency equals the request currency. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-11 | Sensitive permission positive/negative | Actor A has `ProjectPaymentRequest.View` and `PaymentSettlement.Record`; Actor B has the View key only; Actor C has the record key only. | Attempt record from UI and direct API as each actor. | Only A sees and can execute the action. B and C are forbidden; no role grant is seeded automatically. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-12 | Read permission | User has `ProjectSettlement.View`; another user has neither the section View permission nor an assigned-review exception. | Read settlement history in the project and call the API as both users. | Authorized user receives only in-scope history; unauthorized user receives no data and is denied. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-13 | Cross-Project/Tenant isolation | User is scoped to Project A/Tenant A; another Approved request belongs to Project B or Tenant B. | Substitute foreign project/request IDs in list/detail/record routes. | API returns not found/forbidden; composite Tenant/Project foreign keys reject inconsistent associations; no foreign request or audit data is disclosed. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-14 | Annual Budget unchanged | Payment Request has an Approved Annual Budget reservation. | Record a partial and then full settlement; compare budget availability before and after. | Original allocation remains reserved; settlement does not deduct again, release funds, create CostEntry, or create another ledger. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-15 | Financial Activity and audit | Authorized user records a partial payment and the final payment. | Compare request history, `AuditEntries`, and the Financial Activity timeline. | Chronological business-readable events include request/settlement references, approved and payment amounts, currency, date/reporting FY, actor/UTC time, totals/outstanding before/after and status transition; retries add no duplicate event. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-16 | Cross-year payment date and closed-period policy | Request is approved against FY N and settlement date falls in FY N+1 under Tenant calendar; include a formally closed period once Year-End Closing exists. | Record and inspect payment date/reporting FY/original budget association; after Sprint 5 defines closed-period policy, attempt a posting into a closed period. | Settlement reports in FY N+1 while the original request and reservation remain in FY N; no automatic transfer/carry-forward occurs. Closed-period rejection is not verified by Sprint 3 and must be validated against Sprint 5 policy. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT; closed-period scenario depends on Sprint 5 |
| SET-17 | Optional document handling | Existing project document upload/link infrastructure is available or unavailable. | Inspect settlement form and attempt to attach a document if a supported extension exists. | MVP allows a settlement with no attachment; Sprint 3 introduces no separate document reference or access path. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-18 | Historical preservation and planned payments | Database contains old PaymentObligation settlements and payment-request approval history. | Compare source rows, IDs, ownership, totals, audit, and status before/after the additive migration. | Old settlement IDs and amounts remain tied to PaymentObligation; no historical request is assigned or rewritten; both ownership paths share one ledger and no row is deleted. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-19 | Non-Administration first grant does not require actor possession | Same-Tenant authorized role administrator has `Roles.ConfigurePermissions` but does not hold `PaymentSettlement.Record`; prepare a user-managed custom role and an in-scope Project, plus Active Deny and foreign-project cases. | Grant `PaymentSettlement.Record` to the custom role, save, reload, and refresh effective permissions; exercise the settlement action in scope. Revoke the permission, save, reload, and confirm effective access is removed. Repeat with Active Deny and a foreign Project. | An authorized role administrator can grant and revoke this active, assignable, scoped non-Administration permission without personally holding it. Tenant/Project scope and Active Deny remain enforced; no permission is automatic. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-20 | Historical settlements are immutable | Existing Payment Request settlement is persisted. | Attempt direct update/delete and inspect available UI/API actions. | Posted settlement cannot be edited/deleted; Sprint 4 only releases unpaid commitment and does not correct, reverse, or refund a settlement. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-21 | Legacy settlement currency is not invented | Existing PaymentObligation settlement rows predate stored settlement currency; new rows store currency. | Compare historical rows and UI output before/after the additive migration; inspect obligation currency update behavior. | Historical amount/date/reference/owner remain unchanged; no currency is copied from the current obligation; history displays “currency not recorded”; new settlements retain the currency captured at posting; obligation currency cannot be changed after a settlement exists. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |
| SET-22 | Exactly one settlement parent | Database includes legacy PaymentObligation settlements and new Project Payment Request settlements. | Attempt to persist a row with only a valid legacy parent, only a valid request parent, both parents, neither parent, and a mismatched Tenant/Project parent. | The two valid single-parent rows are accepted; both-owner and ownerless rows fail the check constraint; cross-Tenant/Project owners fail composite FKs; no duplicate settlement fact is created. | Deferred to Sprint 7 consolidated UAT |

## Project Payment Request Reversals - Financial Sprint 4

Viewing reversal history requires `ProjectSettlement.View` in authenticated Tenant/Project scope. Creating, editing, submitting, or cancelling a draft requires `ProjectPaymentReversal.Create` and its declared `ProjectPaymentRequest.View` dependency. A decision requires `ProjectPaymentReversal.Approve`, active Project Team eligibility, and assignment to the reversal. No existing role receives sensitive permissions automatically; writes fail closed until explicitly delegated. Reversal releases unpaid commitment only; it is never a cash refund or settlement correction.

| ID | Scenario | Preconditions | UAT steps | Expected result and evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| REV-01 | Create against Approved request | Approved request has an approved Annual Budget, positive unpaid commitment, current Fiscal Year, and active scoped user with `ProjectPaymentRequest.View` and `ProjectPaymentReversal.Create`. | Create a Draft Reversal Request with amount, reason, and idempotency key. | One linked reversal is created with original request, project, Tenant, Annual Budget/FY, requester, amount, and audit event; original request and allocation remain unchanged while Draft. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-02 | Reject non-Approved original | Original request is Draft, Pending, Rejected, or Cancelled. | Attempt to create or submit a reversal for each status. | Every command is rejected; no approved release or successful mutation audit event exists. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-03 | Full reversal of fully unpaid request | Approved request has no settlements or prior approved reversals. | Submit and approve the full remaining amount. | Original allocation becomes zero exactly once; original approved amount/decision remain unchanged; no settlement, CostEntry, or refund is created. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-04 | Partial reversal of unpaid commitment | Approved request has a positive unpaid commitment. | Approve a partial release below the maximum reversible amount. | Active allocation decreases by exactly the approved amount; remaining payable remains nonnegative; original request stays Approved. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-05 | Reversal after partial settlement | Approved request is partially settled. | Request a reversal within `original amount - total settled - previous approved releases`; approve it. | Only the unpaid amount is released; settlement IDs, amounts, dates, currency, and history remain unchanged. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-06 | Reject over-release and fully paid request | Request has known settled/released totals; include a fully settled case. | Submit above the maximum reversible amount and attempt any positive reversal against the fully settled request. | Server rejects both; no allocation change or success audit event occurs. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-07 | Pending/rejected reversal does not release | Reversal is Pending or Rejected. | Compare Annual Budget availability and request effective allocation before and after the decision state. | Only Approved reversals reduce effective allocation; Pending and Rejected values have no budget effect. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-08 | Multiple approved partial reversals | Request retains unpaid commitment after first approved partial release. | Create and approve another partial reversal, then approach and exceed the remaining maximum. | Each approval is counted once; cumulative releases never exceed original amount less paid settlements; the excess is rejected. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-09 | One pending reversal per original | One reversal is Pending for an original request. | Attempt to submit another Draft reversal for that original. | Second submission conflicts; drafts may not create a second concurrently pending workflow. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-10 | Sequential approval | Project approval settings allow Sequential and active eligible approvers are selected in order. | Decide out of order, approve in order, and reject at an intermediate step. | Only active approver acts; rejection stops immediately and no later assignment activates; release occurs only after final approval. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-11 | Parallel Mandatory/Optional approval | Parallel mode has at least one Mandatory assignment. | Test missing Mandatory selection, Optional reject/no-response, all Mandatory approvals, and a Mandatory rejection. | Missing Mandatory assignment is rejected; all Mandatory approvals are required; Optional outcomes do not block; Mandatory rejection prevents release. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-12 | Concurrent settlement and reversal | Same approved request has outstanding amount; settlement and final reversal together would exceed original amount. | Execute both commands concurrently against SQL Server. | Shared Project lock serializes operations; committed paid plus approved released total never exceeds original; loser conflicts and no duplicate audit/financial fact is committed. Capture transaction/SQL evidence. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-13 | Duplicate create and approval retries | Reversal create/decision has committed but response was lost. | Retry create with same idempotency key and payload, then retry approval decision. | Retries return the existing result; no duplicate reversal, decision, release, or audit event. Reusing key with changed payload conflicts. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-14 | Annual Budget availability and no double accounting | Approved reversal exists against an approved Annual Budget and settlements exist. | Compare availability, active allocation, total settled, and CostEntries before/after reversal approval. | Available budget increases by the approved unpaid release only; settlements are not subtracted again; no CostEntry or second reservation ledger appears. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-15 | No settlement refund or mutation | Original request has one or more posted settlements. | Inspect reversal actions/UI and attempt direct refund, settlement edit, or delete. | Sprint 4 offers only unpaid-commitment release; paid amounts remain untouched and no refund/bank/cash transaction is created. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-16 | Tenant/Project scope | Actor and original request belong to Tenant A/Project A; foreign reversal/original IDs belong elsewhere. | Substitute cross-Project and cross-Tenant IDs on read, create, update, decision, and cancel routes. | Foreign records are not disclosed or changed; scope and composite relationship validation rejects the operation. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-17 | Permission positive/negative and assigned approver | One actor has `ProjectPaymentRequest.View` and `ProjectPaymentReversal.Create`; candidate approvers differ in `ProjectPaymentReversal.Approve`; one approver is unassigned/ineligible. | Inspect the reversal approver list; attempt UI and direct API commands with each actor. | Candidate list includes only active eligible Project Team users with the approval permission and exact assignment; only authorized scoped creator can submit; unassigned, inactive, cross-scope, or missing-permission actors are denied. No role receives sensitive permission implicitly. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-18 | Financial Activity and audit integrity | Authorized users perform create, edit, submit, assignment, decision, approval/rejection, cancellation. | Compare AuditEntries and Financial Activity chronology with request/reversal state. | Events include Tenant/Project, references, Annual Budget/FY, currency, actor/UTC time, reason/comment, amount and before/after release/active/payable balances; failed commands do not commit success events. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-19 | Legacy request without verified budget | Approved legacy request lacks AnnualBudgetId, FiscalYear, or reference. | Attempt create, submit, and approve reversal. | All release operations fail closed; no arbitrary FY/budget is inferred and legacy request/settlement history remains unchanged. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-20 | Cross-year and closed period | Request belongs to prior/future FY or to a period later marked closed by Sprint 5. | Attempt reversal creation/submission/approval across FY; repeat after closed-period policy is available. | Sprint 4 blocks a release when budget FY is not current; it never transfers released value across years. Closed-period expectation must follow Sprint 5 policy; no automatic carry-forward. | Deferred to Sprint 7B; closed-period case depends on Sprint 5 |
| REV-21 | EN/AR, RTL, themes | English/Arabic locales and right-to-left mode are available. | Inspect create, history, balances, approval checklist, validation/error, and activity in light/dark modes. | Original/settled/released/active/payable amounts, status, reason, and “unpaid commitment only; no refund” explanation are accurate, localized, and readable in both directions/themes. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-22 | Approved reversal immutability | Reversal is Approved and its release is reflected in the original budget allocation. | Attempt to edit amount/reason, cancel, resubmit, or directly change the effective allocation through API/UI. | Approved reversal history is immutable; only a separately approved additional reversal can further release unpaid commitment; no direct allocation edit or settlement mutation exists. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| REV-23 | Reversal requester self-approval denied by default | Requester is an active Project Team member with `ProjectPaymentReversal.Approve`; no saved Reversal self-approval policy exists. | Confirm the requester is absent from eligible approver lookup; craft a submit selecting the requester; inspect pending review projection/CanDecide and attempt a direct decision against any historical self-assignment. | Requester cannot be selected or submitted as an approver and cannot decide their own reversal; crafted/historical assignments do not expose CanDecide. Rejected operations leave status, assignments, balances, and audit success events unchanged. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |

## Project Year-End Closing and Budget Carry Forward — Financial Sprint 5

Reading statements/history requires `ProjectAnnualBudgetClosing.View`. Preparing, submitting, and cancelling requires `ProjectAnnualBudget.Close`; an approval/rejection requires `ProjectAnnualBudget.CloseApprove` and an assigned eligible active Project Team member. A decision that includes free/reserved carry also requires `ProjectAnnualBudget.CarryForward`. No broad finance alias or write key is a fallback, and no sensitive permission is granted automatically. Runtime scenarios are deferred to Sprint 7B and require API/UI, scope, workflow, audit, and balance evidence.

| ID | Scenario | Preconditions | UAT steps | Expected result and evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FYC-01 | Tenant fiscal boundaries | Tenants use January and a non-January fiscal start month. | Prepare statements for dates on both sides of each boundary and inspect end-year labels and Tenant timezone behavior. | Every period spans 12 months; label uses ending year; timestamps use Tenant timezone/half-open boundaries; no server-local assumption. Capture period/API evidence. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-02 | Project-only close | Project belongs to a Program/Portfolio with sibling Projects. | Close one Project Fiscal Year and inspect all parents/siblings. | Only that Project's budget and period close; no parent or sibling budget/closing is created. Capture scoped records. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-03 | Closing statement reconciliation | Example: FY budget USD 1,000,000, approved allocations USD 200,000, actual payment USD 30,000. | Prepare statement and compare authoritative budget, request, reversal, and settlement rows. | Gross allocations, releases, effective allocation, paid, outstanding USD 170,000, free USD 800,000, total unspent USD 970,000 remain distinct and reconcile. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-04 | Payment reporting by actual date | Request originated in FY N; settlement occurs in FY N+1. | Compare request-origin FY, funding FY, actual settlement date/FY, and closing statement. | Cash paid is attributed to actual payment period; original request association remains unchanged. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-05 | Full, partial, or no free carry | Closing has free available balance and destination values. | Approve amount equal to all, part, or zero free balance. | Only approved explicit amount is carried; non-carried balance is recorded; no carry occurs by default. Compare source snapshot and destination opening. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-06 | Reserved commitment carry | Approved request has unpaid payable balance; request is otherwise valid. | Approve closing with the commitment-transfer choice enabled. | Transfer equals unpaid payable only, preserves request history, removes that portion from source availability and reserves it once in destination. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-07 | Unselected obligation blocks close | Source budget has outstanding approved payable. | Prepare/submit without selecting commitment carry. | Closing cannot submit/approve; no year lock or transfer is written. Capture actionable issue and unchanged balances. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-08 | Multi-year obligation | Request is carried FY N→N+1, partially settled/reversed, then carried FY N+1→N+2. | Inspect each transfer, settlement, release, and current budget assignment after both closings. | Consecutive chain preserves original request and has exactly one current funding assignment; paid/released portions are never transferred or double reserved. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-09 | Historical request/settlement/reversal preservation | Project contains original requests, decisions, settlements and approved reversals. | Compare IDs, amounts, dates, links, statuses and audit history before/after closing. | No original fact is rewritten, duplicated, reassigned to an invented FY, or deleted. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-10 | Destination uniqueness and opening | No destination budget exists, then repeat with an existing Draft and Approved budget. | Complete closing; retry; then prepare another opening with conflicting/exact values. | At most one budget per Project/FY and one close per Project/FY. Exact existing values may be reconciled without overwrite; conflicts fail closed; no duplicate funding. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-11 | Destination capacity and spendability | Destination budget has insufficient free capacity or is Draft. | Attempt transfer and then attempt a new Payment Request before destination approval. | Insufficient capacity blocks closing; Draft destination is not spendable; after approval, reserved carry is included once in allocations and unavailable for new requests. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-12 | No double deduction from settlements | Transferred commitment has partial then full settlement. | Compare annual availability before/after each settlement. | Settlement changes paid/outstanding reporting only; it does not deduct a second time or release the original allocation. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-13 | Settlement after carry | Active destination budget is approved/open and a valid transferred request remains payable. | Record a settlement whose actual date belongs to destination FY, then try a date in another period. | Valid settlement uses destination transfer reference; wrong FY/closed period is rejected; original request FY and source budget remain intact. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-14 | Reversal after carry | Transferred request has an unpaid balance and destination period is current/open. | Approve a partial reversal; try one that exceeds unpaid amount or targets source closed year. | Valid release reduces active destination allocation exactly once; source closed year is not credited; no settlement/refund changes. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-15 | Pending requests/reversals at year-end | Source period contains Pending Payment Request and/or Pending Reversal Request. | Prepare a close with each pending state present. | Closing flags and blocks unresolved operations; no request is automatically approved, moved, or cancelled. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-16 | Unsafe historical data | Include unassociated legacy approvals, missing/unknown currency, conflicting Tenant/Project, invalid amount, or over-allocation. | Prepare and submit closing. | Statement records issues and final close remains blocked; no guessed currency, ownership, period, or transfer is persisted. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-17 | Approval, rejection, and separation | Eligible active team approvers are configured sequentially or in parallel. | Attempt out-of-order decisions, mandatory/optional combinations, preparer self-approval, rejection, and duplicate decision retry. | Shared approval rules are respected; preparer cannot approve; rejection leaves year and money unchanged; required approvals close exactly once; retries do not duplicate transfer/audit events. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-18 | Permission positive/negative/delegation | Actors have read, close, close-approve, carry, and write permissions in different combinations; no automatic grant. | Execute UI and direct API actions as each actor, including unauthorized delegation. | Only the exact permission combination enables each action; unauthorized actors are denied; assigned approver and project scope are required; no role-name bypass/grant occurs. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-19 | Cross-Tenant/Project isolation | User is authorized for Project A/Tenant A; closing/budget/transfer belongs to Project B or Tenant B. | Substitute identifiers on list, prepare, submit, cancel and decide routes. | Foreign data is not disclosed/mutated; composite relationships reject mismatched ownership. Capture API/security and database evidence. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-20 | Concurrent settlement/reversal/closing | Same project request is targeted by settlement/reversal while closing approval runs. | Execute concurrent SQL Server commands using the standard test harness and inspect all commits. | Consistent project lock ordering permits only a valid serialized outcome; no lost payment, duplicate reservation/transfer, negative budget, or partial audit. Runtime SQL evidence is mandatory. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-21 | Duplicate prepare/approval retry | Same idempotency key and decision are retried after response loss. | Replay identical and changed payloads. | Identical preparation returns existing closing; changed payload conflicts; only one closing/transfer/audit event is committed. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-22 | Closed-period immutability | Project FY is Closed and has historical statements/transfers. | Attempt edit, reopen, transfer change, backdated settlement, and reversal release to source; inspect history. | Closed statement and source budget cannot be changed/reopened; no late operation silently credits the closed year; authorized settlement against current carried funding remains valid. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-23 | Audit and Financial Activity | Complete and reject/cancel different closing attempts; complete a carry-forward. | Compare `AuditEntries` and Project Financial Activity events. | Chronological events capture source/destination budgets/FYs, amount/currency, actor/UTC, status and before/after values; failed and retried commands create no duplicate success event. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-24 | EN/AR, RTL and themes | EN and AR accounts, RTL and light/dark settings available. | Inspect statement, preview, history, approvals and errors at desktop/mobile widths. | Labels, amounts, status, actions, loading/empty/error states render correctly in both languages, directions, themes and responsive layouts. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-25 | Legacy Payment Obligation settlement in a closed period | A Planned Payment / Payment Obligation belongs to a Project with one closed and one open Tenant Fiscal Year. | Record a settlement with an actual date in each period through the existing Planned Payments API. | The closed-period write is rejected; an allowed open-period write preserves obligation ownership, amount/date/currency, and shared AuditEntries behavior. No annual budget is deducted or released. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-26 | Legacy settlement versus closing race | A Payment Obligation settlement and year-end approval target the same Project concurrently. | Execute both against SQL Server using the standard concurrency harness. | The common serializable Project-row lock yields a consistent order; no settlement is silently included after close, no close omits a committed payment, and no partial audit event persists. Capture transaction and database evidence. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |
| FYC-27 | Sequential approver order controls | Closing approvers are configured in Sequential mode with at least three eligible Project Team members. | Move approvers up/down in the UI, submit, then attempt decisions out of order. | Displayed order matches persisted positions and only the active approver may decide. Capture EN/AR keyboard-accessible UI and API evidence. | Deferred to Sprint 7B |

## Sprint 7B-01 Core Runtime UAT — executed 2026-11-01

Controlled Development UAT of the Project, Portfolio, Program, and manager-assignment core flows. Identities were configured by effective permission and authenticated scope, never by role name. No permission key, role grant, migration, or seed was added or changed for this execution; the only product-code change was the narrow defect fix D-01 below. Financial capabilities were out of execution scope for this sprint (Section 7 preparation only, see `SPRINT-7B-01-FINANCIAL-PERMISSION-MATRIX-PROPOSAL.md`).

## Cost Utilization Calendar View - 2026-11-01

Daily Detail, Calendar View, and Day Details reuse `Resource.View` and the existing tenant/project management scope. Monetary values are returned only when `Resource.ViewCostRate` is effective. The separate `CostUtilization.Export` operation controls generation through the dedicated export-data API; it also requires `Resource.View`, preserves the same scope, and carries forward cost redaction. No role or protected Admin grant is added automatically. Runtime scenarios remain for consolidated UAT.

Capture request/response and screenshots. | Not run; source implementation and automated tests only. | Deferred |
| CUV-02 | Missing Resource.View | User has no `Resource.View` claim. | Open `/resources/cost-utilization` and call the report API directly. | Route remains guarded and API denies access; no report, activity, or cost data is disclosed. Capture effective permissions and response. | Not run; source implementation and automated tests only. | Deferred |
| CUV-03 | Cost fields redacted | User has `Resource.View` but lacks `Resource.ViewCostRate`. | Open Daily Detail, expand a populated day, open Calendar View, and open a populated Day Details sheet. | Operational hours and utilization remain visible; every monetary value stays hidden/redacted in all three presentations. Capture report `canViewCosts` and UI. | Not run; source implementation and automated tests only. | Deferred |
| CUV-04 | Tenant and Project scope | Two tenants contain similarly named Projects, Resources, activities, or Work Items; user manages only one Project scope. | Load the calendar, open days with work, and attempt the same report request with a foreign Project identifier. | Calendar, cost totals, and activity/work-item labels include only authorized same-tenant/scope records; foreign scope is denied or omitted by the existing API. Capture scope, requests, and responses. | Not run; source implementation and automated tests only. | Deferred |

## Cost Utilization PDF and Excel Export - 2026-11-01

PDF and Excel output is generated from the dedicated `GET /api/resource-analytics/cost-utilization/export-data` endpoint. The endpoint requires both `Resource.View` and `CostUtilization.Export`, applies the same tenant/project management scope as report reads, and preserves cost redaction through `Resource.ViewCostRate`. Team filtering additionally requires `Team.View`. `Resource.View` alone continues to allow report reads without allowing export; `CostUtilization.Export` is not automatically granted to any role or the protected Admin baseline.

**Backup-policy supersession (2026-11-10):** The execution snapshots above retain their historical backup statuses. Development/UAT backups are optional for resets and ordinary authorized operations; missing backup media, independent storage, or `RESTORE VERIFYONLY` does not block work. A destructive reset still requires explicit Product Owner authorization and all target, migration, and schema checks. This policy note does not authorize or imply a reset, migration, or seed. Production backup and recovery safeguards remain mandatory.

## P0-03 Approvals Authorization Scenarios
- Positive: requester submits; approver approves/rejects; delegate to eligible user.
- Negative: missing ApprovalRequest.View -> 403; requester self-approval -> 403; ineligible delegation -> 403; audit failure aborts.
- Cross-scope: no tenant -> 403; cross-tenant record -> 404.

## P1 Project Manager Assignment UX and Handover Enforcement

No permission key or existing role grant is added. Project creation and editing retain `Project.Create` and `Project.Edit` with authenticated tenant/management scope. The dedicated manager candidate route uses the existing `WorkflowLookup.Project.Users` policy (`Project.View`, `Project.Create`, or `Project.Edit`) and returns only active same-tenant users with an active `Management`-category role and effective `Handover.Approve`; create and incoming acceptance revalidate the candidate. Post-creation transfer and first assignment continue to require the existing `Handover.Manage` or `Handover.AdminInitiate` initiation permission. All operations must respect Active Deny and resource scope.

## Risk Action Next Follow-up Notifications - 2026-11-06

These cases cover the Product Owner-approved reminder contract. The API accepts `NextFollowUpDate` as a date-only `yyyy-MM-dd` value and stores it at midnight in the existing persistence type; the user does not choose a notification time. The due instant is the configured start of the effective working day resolved for the Project's Organization. Project calendar selection takes precedence; otherwise the effective Tenant-to-Organization `workingCalendar.defaultCalendarId` setting is used. Holidays and configured workdays determine the next eligible day. No five-day week, fixed hour, server timezone, or implicit calendar is assumed. The API and React builds pass. Backend test compilation passes, but VSTest could not connect to testhost (90 seconds for the focused set; 180 seconds for one test), and no case ran. Vitest was not run after automatic review blocked its telemetry egress. Browser UAT is reserved for the Product Owner. Development database identity, backup, migration history, and schema remain unverified; no database operation or browser UAT was performed.

The existing canonical `Risk.View`, `Risk.Edit`, and settings permissions are reused; no permission key or role grant was added. Current Action and Risk owners resolve to active same-tenant System Users; legacy Resource-to-User links remain compatible and duplicate owners are deduplicated. The Tenant/Organization calendar selector is EN/AR and uses the same-tenant calendar lookup; Organization inheritance resets the override. `Settings.View` is accepted for reading that lookup while existing Resource lookup permissions remain supported, and Active Deny still applies. Notifications navigate using the Risk Action public identifier only; internal database IDs remain behind an authorized target API.

Inspect pending occurrence state and recipient notifications. | Notification becomes due at the configured start of that same Organization working day. No fixed default time or server timezone is used. | Implementation in source; browser UAT not run. | NOT RUN |
| RAF-02 | Non-working date and Organization holiday | Active Action has a follow-up date on a non-working day; include a configured holiday and a calendar whose next working day differs from a five-day-week assumption. | Advance the deterministic clock through the date and to the next working day. | Delivery is scheduled for the next configured working day's own start time, skipping holidays/non-working days. | Implementation in source; browser UAT not run. | NOT RUN |
| RAF-03 | Weekday-specific hours, timezone, and daylight-saving transition | Effective calendar has different starts by weekday and an Organization timezone with a daylight-saving boundary in the test period. | Calculate due instants across each weekday and the daylight-saving transition; compare local calendar time and stored UTC instant. | The actual due day start is used. Timezone conversion is deterministic and valid through daylight-saving gaps/overlaps; server timezone does not affect the result. | Implementation in source; browser UAT not run. | NOT RUN |

## P0 Meeting Minutes display after completion - 2026-11-05

The source-level defect was confirmed on 2026-11-06: a public Meeting route identifier was sent to Minutes APIs that resolve by internal Meeting.Id, causing a valid public route to be treated as missing. The page now resolves Meeting Details first and sends the internal ID to Minutes and child-resource requests. An earlier consolidated React run recorded 8 files / 41 tests; that is historical evidence, not a current run. The latest React production build and typecheck passed. The API and API test-project builds passed (0 errors); focused tests then failed to connect to testhost after 90 seconds, and a one-test retry failed after 180 seconds before any test started. The latest Vitest run was not executed after automatic review blocked telemetry egress. No browser UAT or database operation was performed; the Product Owner will execute browser UAT. The reported /api/meetings 500 could not be reproduced or ruled out without a live API/database connection. Use same-tenant actors with explicit effective permissions and management scope. Do not infer authorization from role names. A successful Minutes GET with JSON null means the authorized Meeting exists but has no Minutes row; it must remain distinct from missing/inaccessible Meeting, 401, 403, and server failure.

## Universal SMTP email notifications — 2026-11-07

The email channel uses an SMTP-only standards connector without provider selection. Profiles resolve Organization → Tenant → Platform, and an explicitly disabled override stops fallback. Supported security modes are required STARTTLS and implicit TLS; username/password is supported, while Trusted Relay requires deployment-owned exact-host and scoped-network approval. OAuth2/XOAUTH2 is visibly unavailable in this release. Email remains opt-in by event preference and uses the existing durable notification delivery worker. `Accepted` means SMTP accepted the submission, not that a recipient mailbox delivered it. Source and focused automated checks do not count as browser or live SMTP UAT. Run against the authorized Development schema and a controlled test relay; never use production recipients or credentials.

The applicable permissions are `EmailNotifications.View`, `EmailNotifications.Manage`, `EmailNotifications.Test`, and `EmailNotifications.Logs`; Organization profile access also requires the existing explicit Organization Settings grant. Platform profile access uses `PlatformSettings.Read` and `PlatformSettings.Write`. Test with separate identities that have the relevant grant, lack it, and have Active Deny. No new email permission is implicitly granted to existing roles or the protected Admin baseline.

| ID | Scenario | Preconditions | UAT steps | Expected result and evidence | Actual result | Status |

Disable Organization A, then repeat; reset its override and repeat. | Resolution is Organization → Tenant → Platform. A disabled exact override stops fallback and cancels queued sends. Reset returns to the next inherited scope. Organization IDs are only used when resolved from the canonical event's owning project/resource. Capture settings and delivery rows. | Focused test source added; test-project build stopped during IKVM conversion before tests compiled. Browser/runtime UAT not run. | NOT RUN |

Active Deny wins even when another grant or role would otherwise permit access. View alone cannot modify, test, or view logs. No permission is inferred from an Admin role. Preserve request/response evidence for allowed and denied cases. | Focused service authorization test source added; build stopped during IKVM conversion before compilation. Browser/API UAT not run. | NOT RUN |

## My Work horizontal tabs and Time Entry Side Sheet - 2026-11-06

My Work presents its existing three sections as horizontal **My Time**, **Approvals**, and **Tasks** tabs, linked to `/my-work/timesheets`, `/my-work/approvals`, and `/my-work/tasks`, using the shared Project Details `RouteTabs` design below each page header. `/my-work` continues to default to Tasks. Existing task status filters and approval Pending/History tabs remain inside their current routes. Add New Time Entry opens the existing create form in a Side Sheet and continues to use the existing Time Tracking API and permission checks. No permission keys, grants, or backend categories were added.

Focused My Work verification passed 6 files / 29 tests. A broader check ran 10 test files: 50 passed and 2 failed (52 total). The CSRF bootstrap mock rejection in `auth-session-isolation.test.tsx` was fixed; remaining failures are its refresh flow expecting a 401 retry to resolve and a `timer.test.tsx` timing assertion expecting `00:01:00` but receiving `00:01:01`. Typecheck, lint, and production build passed; lint reported 10 existing warnings in unrelated files, and build reported existing PlannedPayments mixed dynamic/static import and chunk-size warnings. A separate API log showed `/api/auth/csrf` called over HTTP while antiforgery uses `SecurePolicy=Always`; this auth/API runtime issue was outside this UI task and was not changed here. Browser UAT was not run per request.

## Sprint 1 — Document Center and File Storage Foundation - 2026-11-10

Governance / Document Center is the single catalog for approved Organization templates. Project Documents remains the existing project-document view and may create a project copy from a selected template, but it does not manage the template catalog. Template registration requires effective `Document.Create` and `Document.TemplateManage`; template versioning and activation require `Document.Edit` and `Document.TemplateManage`; template archiving requires `Document.Archive` and `Document.TemplateManage`. Reads use `Document.View`. Every operation remains subject to Active Deny and authenticated Tenant, Organization, Project, and management scope. No new permission is introduced or granted automatically. An uploaded file is not represented as independent verification of an external signoff; no in-system approval workflow is part of Sprint 1.
