Repeat with each user and try a foreign Organization identifier in the API. | Only templates belonging to an active Organization inside the authenticated Tenant and permitted management scope are returned. Organization selection is not authorization evidence. Search/filter results and template name, description, category, version, status, and Organization match server data. | Source now assigns templates to Organizations and scopes selection through Tenant/management context, including Program-to-Portfolio organization resolution. Focused resolver/security regressions are authored but were not run; migration is not applied and API/browser checks were not run. | Pending consolidated technical verification and Product Owner Browser UAT |

## P1 - Document Categories Management

**Reference Data → Document Categories** is the management surface for the existing `DocumentCategory` catalog. It retains stable category IDs, Tenant/Organization ownership, template associations, versions, and audit history. Users can search, create, edit, and activate/deactivate categories within an Organization they can access. Categories are deactivated instead of deleted, so historical template references remain readable. This taxonomy is descriptive/filtering metadata; Phase Gate requirements continue to pin approved Template IDs and Template Version IDs.

Governance / Document Center remains the approved, colorful Document Library. Its category folders and filters consume the same managed catalog. The Register Approved Template selector obtains active categories from `GET /api/document-categories/template-options?organizationId=` using the existing `Document.Create` + `Document.TemplateManage` operation permissions; template registration does not require broad `DocumentCategory.View` listing access. The permission-gated **Manage Categories** shortcut links to `/administration/reference-data/document-categories`; the old management tab is not part of the Library.

The canonical category permissions are `DocumentCategory.View`, `DocumentCategory.Create`, `DocumentCategory.Edit`, and `DocumentCategory.ActivateDeactivate`, with English and Arabic labels in Roles & Permissions. They remain Tenant-scoped and grantable across eligible tenant Role Categories. Backend and UI operations preserve Active Deny and Tenant/Organization boundaries. Tenant Admin receives no implicit permission baseline: its effective access comes from explicit grants, and Active Deny applies. A display name or broad `pmo.admin` role claim grants no bypass. Development grants require a separate live verification.

### Prior category verification - 2026-11-10

The prior source-only category pass reported an offline EF model/snapshot gate synchronized against 21 migration sources. Development SQL inspection was blocked by SSPI (`Cannot generate SSPI context`); the live target, migration history, and Admin grants were therefore unknown. Backend focused tests were blocked before execution by existing API test-project compile errors; the prior Document Center parity run passed 8/8, and the prior React typecheck passed before a later test-mock-only edit. These results do not verify the Reference Data move or the current worktree.

### Reference Data integration verification - 2026-11-11

Focused React suites passed **34/34**, and `npm run typecheck` passed. The single API build/test attempt failed during output copy with `MSB3021/MSB3027` because API output DLLs were locked by another process; there was no retry. The official offline EF model gate passed against 21 repository migrations. Read-only inspection using the `appsettings.Development` endpoint `tcp:localhost,1433` / `NexusPmo` failed before connection with `TCP Provider error 0: Access is denied`; this configured endpoint is not verified SQL identity. SQL Server identity, database ID, `__EFMigrationsHistory`, physical schema, actual pending migration count/names, category rows, and live Admin grants remain **not verified**. Persistence status: backup **Not requested**; migrations **none by this resume**, with no SQL/migration script generated/applied; seed and persistence tests **not run**. No database writes were made. Live Admin grants and authenticated browser UAT remain pending.

### Category Save follow-up - 2026-11-11

The Product Owner reports that Save Category does not work. Source verification confirms the form posts the selected Organization and bilingual names to the existing managed-category endpoint, and the shared API client accepts the endpoint's JSON `201 Created` response. Focused Save/page/API React regressions passed **25/25** and `npm run typecheck` passed. The Development migration `20261110210000_AddOrganizationManagedDocumentCategories` is recorded as unapplied in the implementation report, but current live migration history/schema could not be verified after the read-only SQL connection failed. A missing schema migration is therefore a likely API-side cause only if the live request returns HTTP 500; runtime cause remains unconfirmed. No database writes were performed. Product Owner browser/API UAT must capture the POST result and complete after target/schema verification.

### Latest P0 category Save recovery - 2026-11-11

**P0 is not closed.** The official offline EF model gate passed against 21 repository migrations. Read-only inspection using the `appsettings.Development` endpoint `tcp:localhost,1433` / `NexusPmo` failed before connection with `TCP Provider error 0: Access is denied`. This endpoint is configuration evidence only, not verified SQL identity. SQL Server identity, database ID, `__EFMigrationsHistory`, physical schema, actual pending migration count/names, category rows, and live Admin grants are **not verified**. No SQL or migration script was generated/applied; no database writes were made; backup is **Not requested**; seed and persistence tests were not run.

The source `POST /api/document-categories` payload contains the selected `organizationId`, trimmed `nameEn`/`nameAr`, and optional `description`. The actual authenticated HTTP status/body and SQL exception are **not captured**; a 500 is not confirmed. Browser automation stopped because the current URL could not be determined confidently, and repository/temp log search found no category exception. Source authorization and Organization guards are intact, while live grants/scope remain unverified; root cause is unconfirmed. The previous 25/25 React/API-contract tests and typecheck are historical source evidence only. Migration review found `20261110210000_AddOrganizationManagedDocumentCategories` additive (normalized-English backfill, nullable OrganizationId, new fields/index/FK), but target-specific SQL safety review is incomplete. It is the last migration in the chain; a target at migration 17 would require the preceding three migrations in order, but the target state is unknown and must not be assumed.

## Sprint 3 - Project Lifecycle and Phase Gate Enforcement - 2026-11-10

Sprint 3 adds project-scoped snapshots and evidence references that pin the Organization configuration version and exact Document Center template/version. Readiness and transition authorization are separate. `NotConfigured` creates no snapshot and does not block an otherwise authorized phase change. Missing, mismatched, inaccessible, or invalid mandatory evidence is reported as incomplete but does not block the transition; optional requirements also do not block. All evidence is assumed to have received any required external approval before upload; the system does not verify signatures or route approvals. API regression bodies are authored but did not run because the shared test project failed compilation; no authenticated browser/runtime result is claimed.

| ID | Scenario | Preconditions | UAT steps | Expected result | Current evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

Client phase overrides are ignored/rejected. Project Status remains independent. Existing phase values, including null, are not backfilled or silently rewritten by ordinary updates. | Source review only; regression source is being prepared. API tests and runtime create/import/bulk checks are Deferred. | Deferred |
| LC-02 | Organization configuration snapshot and history pinning | Organization has a versioned Pre-Initiation → Initiation configuration with exact template versions; project has no prior snapshot. | Read readiness, save a new Organization configuration version, then read the project checklist and history again. | The first configured checklist read pins one immutable project snapshot and requirement metadata to the applicable configuration/version. Later Organization edits do not silently replace that project's requirement set. No configuration yields `NotConfigured` and no snapshot. | Source contracts identify per-project snapshot and no-config behavior; service/API regression and runtime checks are Deferred. | Deferred |
| LC-03 | Mandatory and Optional evidence evaluation | Snapshot has one Mandatory and one Optional requirement with pinned Template ID/Version ID. | Leave both absent, link only a valid Mandatory document version, then try a document from another template/version. | Missing Mandatory evidence makes readiness Incomplete and blocks transition. Missing, inaccessible, deleted, invalid, or unreadable references cannot satisfy Mandatory requirements and are returned as structured blockers where authorized. The focused frontend files `document-center-parity.test.tsx`, `project-phase-gate-side-sheet.test.tsx`, and `audit-phase-gate-localization.test.tsx` passed 8/8 after a single retry with TEMP/TMP redirected. `npm run typecheck` passed once with exit 0. API regression test bodies and the full frontend suite were not executed. For migration source generation only, the isolated DesignTime build first failed with 3 compile errors, then an EF scaffold build failed with 2 mapping errors; after fixes, `dotnet build apps/api/tools/NEXUS.Persistence.DesignTime/NEXUS.Persistence.DesignTime.csproj --no-restore -m:1` succeeded with 8 `NU1900` warnings and `dotnet ef migrations add AddProjectPhaseGateLifecycle ... --no-build` generated source migration `20261110190107_AddProjectPhaseGateLifecycle`. The source preserves the existing `AK_Projects_TenantId_Id` key; no SQL script or database operation followed. At Product Owner request, `dotnet build apps/api/src/NEXUS.Api/NEXUS.Api.csproj --no-restore -m:1` then succeeded with 360 warnings and 0 errors after fixing CS0136 in `PortfolioManagementService.cs`; warnings included NU1900 and IKVM diagnostics. No retry was run. API/backend/storage tests, full frontend suite, EF synchronization gate, DB inspection/application, and Product Owner browser UAT remain Deferred. A Product Owner-provided API runtime trace now records `/api/documents` failing with SQL error 207 for missing `Documents.OrganizationId`; this is not an independently authenticated verification, and the exact target/database history remain unchecked. The Product Owner also reported a failed Vite HMR WebSocket and an i18n context error; source nesting is correct, but the stale HMR module-graph explanation remains unverified without a live Vite runtime. Sprint 1 EF model/snapshot confirmation and unapplied Sprint 1/2/3 migrations remain blockers. The Sprint 3 migration and designer are present but unapplied; migration history and schema synchronization remain unverified. Do not mark database readiness or Production readiness from source changes alone.

## Project Documents GET 404 Recovery - 2026-11-11

Project Details -> Documents calls `GET /api/documents?projectId=<route id>`. The backend `ProjectPublicIdRoutingMiddleware` resolves the external `projectId` query value as `Project.PublicId` and returns the confirmed `404` with detail `The requested resource was not found.` when an internal `Project.Id` is supplied. The frontend `ProjectDocumentsTab` was passing `project.id` (internal) instead of the route/public project identifier, so every Project Documents load failed before reaching the Document controller. The frontend now passes the route `projectId` (public) for document list/category filters while retaining `project.id` (internal) for upload payloads and `forProjectId` template lookups. No backend, database, migration, authorization, upload, download, template, or Phase Gate behavior was changed.

| ID | Scenario | Preconditions | UAT steps | Expected result and evidence | Actual result | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PD-404-01 | Existing authorized project with documents | Authorized user with `Document.View` and project scope for an existing project that has documents. | Open Project Details -> Documents. | `GET /api/documents?projectId=<public id>` returns `200` with the project's paginated documents. | Frontend now sends the route/public project id; focused React parity test passed. Runtime API/browser verification pending. | Pending consolidated technical verification and Product Owner Browser UAT |
| PD-404-02 | Existing authorized project without documents | Authorized user with project scope for an existing project with no documents. | Open Project Details -> Documents. | `GET /api/documents?projectId=<public id>` returns `200` with an empty paginated result, not `404`. | Middleware resolves the public id and Document controller returns empty list; no broad catch introduced. Runtime verification pending. | Pending consolidated technical verification and Product Owner Browser UAT |
| PD-404-03 | Invalid project identifier | Authenticated user supplies an internal/nonexistent project id in the external `projectId` position. | Call `GET /api/documents?projectId=<internal or unknown id>`. | Non-disclosing `404` from `ProjectPublicIdRoutingMiddleware`; no Document data or storage access. | Middleware rejects internal/unknown public ids by design; existing middleware tests cover this. | Verified by source and focused middleware tests |
| PD-404-04 | Unauthorized or out-of-scope project | Authenticated user lacks project scope for the requested project. | Open Project Details -> Documents for an out-of-scope project. | Access denied without information leakage; no cross-tenant existence disclosure. | Management scope checks remain unchanged and run after public-id resolution. | Pending consolidated technical verification and Product Owner Browser UAT |
| PD-404-05 | Pagination and filters preserved | Authorized project with multiple document pages/templates. | Navigate pages and use search/category/template filters. | `page`, `pageSize`, `templatesOnly`, `includeArchived`, `search`, and category filters remain compatible; public route id is translated to internal before controller execution. | Frontend change preserves all query fields; focused parity test passed. | Pending consolidated technical verification and Product Owner Browser UAT |
| PD-404-06 | Project identifier contract | Project route uses `Project.PublicId`; document API requires the public id externally. | Inspect network request and middleware resolution. | Route/public id is sent to `/api/documents`; internal id is used only for upload payloads and `forProjectId` template lookups. | React typecheck passed; focused parity test updated to distinguish public and internal ids and passed. | Verified by source and focused React regression |

## P1 Dynamic Project Phases and Statuses — 2026-11-12

Project Phase and Project Status are separate persistent catalogs with stable ID-backed project relationships. Catalog Create/Edit requires no workflow or transition-step setup. Status entries carry configurable `ReportingCategory` (`InProgress`, `Completed`, or `Excluded`) and `AttentionLevel` (`None`, `NeedsAttention`, or `Critical`) semantics for rollups and attention summaries. Project Status changes use the existing `ProjectStatus.Change` permission, scope and audit contract without a transition graph. Phase Gate document readiness remains visible and audited, but missing or mismatched evidence does not block an otherwise authorized Project Phase change. A phase change requires `Project.View` and `ProjectPhase.Change`; `Document.View` is not required for the change. Historical project values, IDs, snapshots, and audit events must remain readable. No hard-delete permission is defined; referenced catalog records are deactivated. Excluded statuses remain selectable if active, but are omitted from active/completed rollups and Phase Gate project scope. This section is a Product Owner UAT handover; every browser/runtime case remains NOT RUN until performed.

Try actions with missing permission and Active Deny. | Each catalog saves with no transition graph. Stable IDs/codes remain unchanged. ### Canonical permission contract

| Operation | Permission | Scope / dependency |
|---|---|---|
| View phase catalog | `ProjectLifecyclePhase.View` | Tenant/authorized Organization catalog scope |
| Create, edit, order/defaults | `ProjectLifecyclePhase.Create` / `ProjectLifecyclePhase.Edit` | Tenant/authorized Organization scope; actions depend on matching View; no transition setup |
| Activate/deactivate phase | `ProjectLifecyclePhase.ActivateDeactivate` | Tenant/authorized Organization scope; does not delete references |
| View status catalog | `ProjectLifecycleStatus.View` | Tenant/authorized Organization catalog scope |
| Create, edit, reporting category/attention level, order/defaults | `ProjectLifecycleStatus.Create` / `ProjectLifecycleStatus.Edit` | Tenant/authorized Organization scope; actions depend on matching View; no transition setup |
| Activate/deactivate status | `ProjectLifecycleStatus.ActivateDeactivate` | Tenant/authorized Organization scope; does not delete references |
| Change project Phase | `Project.View` + `ProjectPhase.Change` | Project scope; readiness is recorded but incomplete document evidence does not block the transition |
| View Phase Gate readiness/evidence | `Project.View` + `Document.View` | Project scope; does not authorize a phase change |
| Create/update a phase-to-category mapping | `ProjectLifecyclePhase.Create` / `ProjectLifecyclePhase.Edit` | Exact Tenant and nullable Organization compatibility; category discovery separately requires `DocumentCategory.View` |
| Change project Status | `ProjectStatus.Change` | Project scope; direct active in-scope catalog selection and audit; no transition graph |
| Load project search options | `Project.View` | Tenant/authorized Organization lookup scope |
| Load Create/Edit options | `Project.Create` or `Project.Edit` | Tenant/authorized Organization lookup scope; does not grant catalog administration |
| Load Status Report status options | `StatusReport.View`, `StatusReport.Create`, `StatusReport.Edit`, or `StatusReport.Publish` | Requires a scoped project ID; returns statuses only |
| Load phase change options | `Project.View` + `ProjectPhase.Change` | Requires a scoped project ID; returns phases only |
| Load status change options | `ProjectStatus.Change` | Requires a scoped project ID; returns statuses only |

Any new reporting-semantics migration from this P1 pass must be checked through the documented host-side EF gate and is not considered applied until verified in current migration history. This cleanup does not authorize a reset, direct SQL patch, or production operation. Product Owner UAT remains the final authenticated runtime validation.

## Organization-owned Role membership and collection scope — 2026-11-16

Role assignments are the source of Organization membership. Collection visibility is permission-scoped per active Organization and remains separate from role-name claims and direct resource assignments.

| ID | Scenario | Preconditions | UAT steps | Expected result | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RBAC-ORG-01 | Derive membership from active assigned Roles | Same-tenant active user; active Organizations with distinct IDs, including two with the same display name; assign one or more Organization-owned Roles. | Inspect `/api/auth/me` and the user administration view; remove one of two Roles in the same Organization, then remove the final Role. | Membership is keyed by Organization ID and requires an active, non-deleted Organization plus an active assignment and Role. Removing one Role preserves membership while another remains; removing the final Role removes it. Equal names never merge Organizations. | Source and regression cases authored; authenticated runtime verification pending |
| RBAC-ORG-02 | Inactive and deleted Organization membership | Assign a user an Organization-owned Role, then deactivate or soft-delete that Organization. | Read `/api/auth/me`, request an Organization-scoped list, and attempt an Organization configuration route. | Inactive/deleted Organizations contribute no displayed membership or permissions and cannot be used as an authorization scope. Tenant-scoped permissions remain independently evaluated. | Source and regression cases authored; authenticated runtime verification pending |
| RBAC-ORG-03 | Project, Portfolio, and Program collection row scope | User has `Project.View`, `Portfolio.View`, or `Program.View` in Organization A; user also has a direct management assignment to a resource in Organization B. Prepare same-tenant resources in both Organizations. | Call each collection GET with no `organizationId`; repeat with Active Deny or without the required View permission. | Organization A's effective grant exposes only A-owned rows. Direct assignment in B does not make B rows visible through a permission claim from A. Active Deny and missing permission return no collection data. | Source and cross-Organization regression cases authored; authenticated runtime verification pending |
| RBAC-ORG-04 | Explicit Organization collection scope | User has scope in two active Organizations and a View grant in only one. | Call each marked collection GET with the permitted, unpermitted, inactive, foreign-Tenant, and malformed `organizationId` values. | A valid requested Organization is verified and limits both permission evaluation and returned rows to that Organization. Invalid, inactive, foreign, or out-of-scope IDs disclose no data. | Source and request-scope regression cases authored; authenticated runtime verification pending |
| RBAC-ORG-05 | Tenant-level View grants preserve existing scope | User has a tenant-scoped effective View permission and existing management scope across multiple Organizations. | Call the marked collection GET without an Organization context and compare returned IDs with the existing management scope. | The tenant-level grant continues to use the existing management scope; Organization-scoped claims do not widen tenant or project scope. | Source regression case authored; authenticated runtime verification pending |
| RBAC-ORG-06 | Legacy membership table removal data gate | Development database identity and schema history verified; inventory every `UserOrganizations` row and dependency before migration review. | Compare each active legacy row to current Role-derived membership; inspect creation/audit metadata and inbound references; review the generated migration SQL and exact hash. | Apply only when removal loses no required/unowned data and all mandatory approvals are present. If metadata or unmatched rows would be lost, keep the migration pending and preserve the table until a reviewed non-lossy disposition is approved. | Development inspection found two active rows with matching derived membership; table-drop migration remains unapplied because it would discard row creation metadata |

## P0 Create Project Lifecycle Options 409 Recovery — 2026-11-17

New Projects require an explicitly selected active Portfolio. The Organization is derived from that Portfolio, and an optional Program must be active, in scope, and owned by the selected Portfolio. Create options must use only that Organization's active lifecycle catalog; a tenant-level Pre-Initiation row is not a fallback. Keep the options GET non-mutating.

| ID | Scenario | Preconditions | UAT steps | Expected result | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CPL-409-01 | Create form before Portfolio selection | Actor has effective `Project.Create`; Create Project form is open; no Portfolio is selected. | Inspect the lifecycle lookup network requests and form state. Attempt submit after supplying other required fields. | No lifecycle-options request is made before Portfolio selection; no false 409 is shown; form asks for a Portfolio and sends no Project or Organization ID. Project creation is blocked until a Portfolio is selected. | Source and test cases authored; runtime verification pending |
| CPL-409-02 | Active scoped Portfolio and Organization lifecycle | Actor has effective `Project.Create` and `Scope.PortfolioIds` contains the selected active Portfolio; its same-Tenant Organization is active and has protected active default Pre-Initiation plus active statuses. | Select the Portfolio, inspect the options request/response, and create a Project with the returned phase/status IDs. | API derives Organization from the selected Portfolio, returns only that Organization's active phases/statuses, and accepts the protected phase. Project is persisted to the selected Portfolio; no caller-supplied Organization scope is required. | Source and test cases authored; runtime verification pending |
| CPL-409-03 | Tenant and unrelated Organization catalog isolation | Selected Portfolio resolves to Organization A; include tenant-level and Organization B phase/status rows, including a tenant-level Pre-Initiation default. | Load create options for Organization A's Portfolio; compare every returned ID with persisted reference data. | Only Organization A's active rows are returned. Tenant-level and Organization B rows do not satisfy the required protected phase and never appear as fallback options. | Source and test cases authored; database comparison pending |
| CPL-409-04 | Program matching and mismatch | Actor can access the selected Portfolio and Program; include a Program owned by it and active, foreign-Portfolio, out-of-scope, and inactive Program cases. | Load options with the matching Program, then substitute each invalid Program ID; attempt Project creation with a mismatched pair. | Matching active Program is accepted. Mismatch, inactive, out-of-scope, foreign-Tenant, or Program-only requests are rejected without disclosing foreign records or creating a Project. | Source and test cases authored; runtime verification pending |
| CPL-409-05 | Portfolio scope and lifecycle phase missing | Include an active same-Tenant Portfolio outside `Scope.PortfolioIds` and an authorized Portfolio whose Organization lacks active default Pre-Initiation. | Request lifecycle options for both and inspect storage before/after. | Unassigned Portfolio is not found. Missing protected phase returns the expected conflict for that Organization; GET does not create or repair any catalog row and does not use tenant fallback. | Source and test cases authored; database and runtime verification pending |
| CPL-409-06 | Organization-owned Role and Side Menu regression | Existing Organization-owned Role and menu assignments are recorded for the test actor. | Run the Role configuration and Side Menu permission regression checks before and after opening Create Project. | Role ownership, explicit grants/denies, menu visibility, and scoped navigation remain unchanged; lifecycle candidate visibility grants no new authority. | Automated execution and Browser UAT pending |

## P0 Lifecycle Bootstrap and Team Capacity 403 — 2026-11-17 follow-up

The authenticated ManagementProvider bootstrap fetches perspective availability; it must not trigger an unscoped lifecycle catalog read through the Projects page. General lifecycle options require an explicit Organization or an in-scope Project. Create Project retains its Portfolio-derived Organization path. Team capacity remains protected by both `Project.View` and `Resource.View`; effective grants and Active Deny are evaluated for the current request and Organization.

| ID | Scenario | Preconditions | UAT steps | Expected result | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CPL-409-07 | Authenticated bootstrap and unscoped options rejection | Authenticated user can open the Project list; no lifecycle scope is selected. | Sign in, inspect requests during ManagementProvider initialization and Project list load. Separately call non-create `/api/project-lifecycle/options` without Organization or Project scope. | Bootstrap/list issues no unscoped lifecycle-options request. Direct unscoped lookup is rejected with 400 validation response before protected-phase checks; valid Organization/Project-scoped lookups retain existing safeguards. | Source and focused automated regressions authored; Browser UAT pending |
| CAP-403-01 | Authorized Project Team Capacity | Actor has current effective `Project.View` and `Resource.View` for the Project's Organization and a valid management assignment/context containing the Project. | Open the persisted Project; inspect shell, overview, and team-capacity requests and response. Repeat with an Active Deny on either permission. | With both permissions and scope, team capacity returns 200. A missing permission or Active Deny remains 403; project ownership/scope is not widened. | Source and focused automated regressions authored; authenticated API/Browser UAT pending |
| CAP-403-02 | Graceful denied Team Capacity | Actor can open the Project but lacks effective `Resource.View`, or the request receives 403 after a stale client snapshot. | Open the Project overview, observe the capacity panel and network requests, then navigate away/back once. | UI shows a localized unavailable state without a retry action or repeated automatic request; any cached capacity data is hidden after 403. Direct API remains denied. | Frontend regression authored; Browser UAT pending |
| CAP-403-03 | Cross-project scope denial | Actor has both view permissions but the target Project is outside the actor's current management scope. | Request the Project shell and team-capacity endpoint using its public ID. | Project scope enforcement returns a non-disclosing not-found result; no team-capacity data is returned. | Source trace complete; authenticated API UAT pending |

## Document Center — Organization and Category selection for template uploads (2026-11-17)

Register Approved Template starts with required Organization and Document Category fields. Organization choices come from the user's active management scope; Category options use the existing active category lookup for the selected Organization. Project Document upload loads its category options from the authorized Project's Organization. Both upload lookups include active Organization-owned and eligible Tenant-shared categories without requiring category-administration permission. The API validates scope and stores the selected category with the uploaded document/version.

| ID | Scenario | Preconditions | UAT steps | Expected result | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

Inspect field order and available Organizations. Select Organization A and a Category, then switch to B. | Organization is first and Category second. Only in-scope active Organizations appear. Category is disabled until Organization is selected; changing Organization clears the prior Category and loads active Organization-owned and eligible Tenant-shared options for the new Organization. | Focused frontend regressions passed: 17 tests across the Document Center and category API files, including field order, scope filtering, disable, clear, reload, and request IDs. |
| DOC-TPL-02 | Required selection and successful association | Authorized actor has an active Organization with an active Organization-owned or eligible Tenant-shared Document Category and a supported file. | Submit with valid Organization, Category, and file. Reload the saved template. Also attempt API submissions without Organization or Category. | Valid upload succeeds and the saved template retains the selected OrganizationId and CategoryId. Missing Organization or Category is rejected before file storage. | Frontend upload assertions pass and `NEXUS.Modules.Documents` builds. Backend persistence tests were added but the API test project did not reach test execution during its IKVM/Mpp adapter rebuild; runtime verification is pending. |
| DOC-TPL-03 | Cross-Organization and Tenant isolation | Include a category owned by another Organization, a Tenant-shared category, an Organization outside the actor's management scope, and a foreign-Tenant category. | Use the Tenant-shared category for an in-scope Organization. Unauthorized Organizations, foreign, inactive, deleted, and mismatched categories are rejected without creating a template or storing file bytes. | Backend positive and negative cases are authored. Documents module build passed; the focused API tests did not execute because the test-project build remained in transitive IKVM/Mpp generation. Authenticated API and browser UAT remain pending. |
| DOC-TPL-04 | Loading, empty, API error, EN/AR, and RTL | Authorized actor can use English and Arabic locales; include an Organization with no active categories and one whose lookup can return an API error. | Open the form, observe loading, empty, and error states; use Retry after an API error; change Organization; submit a valid upload in both locales. | Loading, no available categories, and API errors have distinct localized messages. Retry reissues the lookup; RTL layout remains intact. | Frontend regressions cover loading, empty, API error, and Retry.
